# p3fes_game_tests: Phase 5 replacements (game/) against their recompiled bodies.
#
# The functions in game/replaced.txt are emitted from the user's ELF at build time with
# `--replace game/replaced.txt` (tests/rt's p3fes_rt_emitted_lib), so the program holds both the
# hand-written fn_<name>_<addr> (p3fes_game) and the recompiled fn_<name>_<addr>__recomp. Each test
# runs both on identical guest machines and requires the same result, the same callee-saved
# registers, the same memory outside the dead stack, the same virtual cycles and the same number of
# check points (ADR-0013). Skips when the build had no ELF.

set(_vendor ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/vendor)

add_executable(p3fes_game_tests
    ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/rt/test_main.cpp
    pair_harness.h
    write_tracker.h

    test_replacements.cpp

# p3fes_formats: the host-side BMD parser, the independent reference of test_bmd_msg.cpp.
target_link_libraries(p3fes_game_tests PRIVATE p3fes_common_options p3fes_game p3fes_rt p3fes_recomp_lib p3fes_formats p3fes_native_input p3fes_native_sound
                      p3fes_native_movies)
target_compile_definitions(p3fes_game_tests PRIVATE P3FES_ELF_DEFAULT="${P3FES_RT_EXEC_ELF}")

    # recompiled callees the __recomp bodies and the hand-written versions still call: strlen 0x524388,
    # strcmp 0x524128, strcpy 0x524270, strcat 0x523e68, memcpy 0x521250, rfdir_toUpper 0x56b980,
    # rfdirStrcmpDirName 0x56ba48, and the script VM's K_Assert / K_Abort stubs sub_19D3F0 / sub_19D400
    # (`jr $ra` in retail; the handlers call them on a failed check), and datCalcGetSkillElement 0x3082F0
    # (called by the hand-written datCalcGetElementForStatus through guest::call).
    # (`jr $ra` in retail; the handlers call them on a failed check), and datCalcGetSkillElement sub_3082F0

    # sub_3A6CA0, and the text-node release sub_3B0170 with its fade check frFontLineFadeStep (the tests keep its free path off).
    # (called by the hand-written sub_308120 through guest::call), and frFontCacheGlyph 0x3B0430 (called by
    # the hand-written frFontCreateGlyphNode).
    # (called by the hand-written sub_308120 through guest::call). test_script_vm2.cpp adds CodeFunc_Comm
    # 0x35cf20 / CodeFunc_Run 0x35d2c0 (dispatched by scrTraceCode), scrStartScript 0x35b570 (driven only
    # into its early K_Abort returns), the kwln accessors 0x195460/520/530/540, MT_Misc_BitChk/BitOn/BitOff

    p3fes_rt_emitted_lib(p3fes_game_gen "${_replaced},${_callees}" 0 ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/game/replaced.txt "${_stand_in_hooks}")
    target_link_libraries(p3fes_game_gen PUBLIC p3fes_game)
    target_link_libraries(p3fes_game_tests PRIVATE p3fes_game_gen)
    # The replaced functions' entry points, each asking the installed stand-ins first (recomp/README.md "Forwarders",
    # game/forwarders.cmake): a test can stand in a replaced callee. p3fes_game holds no plain forwarders (those are
    # p3fes_game_forwarders, which only the game links), so these are the executable's only definitions.
    target_sources(p3fes_game_tests PRIVATE ${P3FES_GAME_FORWARDERS_TEST})
    set_source_files_properties(${P3FES_GAME_FORWARDERS_TEST} PROPERTIES GENERATED TRUE
        INCLUDE_DIRECTORIES ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR} COMPILE_OPTIONS /bigobj)
    add_dependencies(p3fes_game_tests p3fes_game_forwarders_gen)
    target_compile_definitions(p3fes_game_tests PRIVATE P3FES_GAME_HAVE_GENERATED=1)
else()
    message(STATUS "tests/game: ${P3FES_RT_EXEC_ELF} not found; p3fes_game_tests is built without recompiled code and skips")
endif()

# ctest entries: by default one per test file (doctest --source-file), so `ctest -j N` runs the files in parallel
# (the gate's suite phase; tools/ours/gate/gate.ps1 adds up their doctest summaries and checks the sum against the
# binary's own --count). Running the binary directly still runs every case in one process.
# -DP3FES_GAME_TESTS_SHARDED=OFF registers the single whole-binary entry instead (the pre-2026-10-20 arrangement).
option(P3FES_GAME_TESTS_SHARDED "p3fes_game_tests: one ctest entry per test file (OFF: one entry for the whole binary)" ON)
if(P3FES_GAME_TESTS_SHARDED)
    # Rough run times (s; the slower of MSVC and clang-cl Release, 2026-10-20) of the longest entries: ctest -j starts
    # the costliest first on a tree without its own measurements yet (a fresh gate tree); later runs use ctest's.
    set(_game_test_costs field_big=250 battle_cmd_menu=240 battle_actions=180 ui3=60 field_hi=30 render_game=25 ui2=20 battle_data=11 battle_calc=10 battle_rest=10 battle_task=9
        render_mesh=9 render_rw2=6 comm_ai=5 render_im2d=4)
    get_target_property(_game_sources p3fes_game_tests SOURCES)
    foreach(_src IN LISTS _game_sources)
        get_filename_component(_file "${_src}" NAME)
        if(_file MATCHES "^test_(.+)\\.cpp$" AND NOT _file STREQUAL "test_main.cpp")
            set(_name ${CMAKE_MATCH_1})
            add_test(NAME p3fes_game_tests.${_name} COMMAND p3fes_game_tests --source-file=*${_file})
            set(_cost 1)
            foreach(_c IN LISTS _game_test_costs)
                if(_c MATCHES "^${_name}=(.+)$")
                    set(_cost ${CMAKE_MATCH_1})
                endif()
            endforeach()
            set_tests_properties(p3fes_game_tests.${_name} PROPERTIES COST ${_cost})
        endif()
    endforeach()
else()
    add_test(NAME p3fes_game_tests COMMAND p3fes_game_tests)
endif()

# p3fes_handgen self-test: regenerates the functions of handgen_selftest.txt and pairs them with their recompiled bodies.
include(${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/handgen_selftest.cmake)
# The faithfulness audit (opt-in, -DP3FES_AUDIT_FAITHFUL=ON): every hand-written function against its recompiled body.
include(${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/audit_faithful.cmake)
